    pAccelerationStructures          const VkAccelerationStructureNV* pAccelerationStructures

struct VkWriteDescriptorSetInlineUniformBlock
    sType                            VkStructureType sType
    pNext                            const void* pNext
    dataSize                         uint32_t dataSize
    pData                            const void* pData
alias  VkWriteDescriptorSetInlineUniformBlockEXT            = VkWriteDescriptorSetInlineUniformBlock

struct VkWriteDescriptorSetPartitionedAccelerationStructureNV
    sType                            VkStructureType sType
    pNext                            void* pNext
    accelerationStructureCount       uint32_t accelerationStructureCount
    pAccelerationStructures          const VkDeviceAddress* pAccelerationStructures

struct VkWriteDescriptorSetTensorARM
    sType                            VkStructureType sType
    pNext                            const void* pNext
    tensorViewCount                  uint32_t tensorViewCount
    pTensorViews                     const VkTensorViewARM* pTensorViews

struct VkWriteIndirectExecutionSetPipelineEXT
    sType                            VkStructureType sType
    pNext                            const void* pNext
    index                            uint32_t index
    pipeline                         VkPipeline pipeline

struct VkWriteIndirectExecutionSetShaderEXT
    sType                            VkStructureType sType
    pNext                            const void* pNext
    index                            uint32_t index
    shader                           VkShaderEXT shader

struct VkXYColorEXT
    x                                float x
    y                                float y

struct VkXcbSurfaceCreateInfoKHR
    sType                            VkStructureType sType
    pNext                            const void* pNext
    flags                            VkXcbSurfaceCreateFlagsKHR flags
    connection                       xcb_connection_t* connection
    window                           xcb_window_t window
